# merged by `run.py --job ownlines merge`. Format in src/world/folk/voice.rs. The code's own wording is each part's fallback.

Our graduates run businesses, coach, and work in offices all over the country.

Here, we prepare you for forty years after football, not just four on the field.
Former players keep calling our staff to hire the next group. That says a lot.
Your {pos} career will end someday, {name}. Your career after it starts here.
Alumni from this team show up in every industry you can name.
We'll set you up for the real game, the one after football.
This program takes care of you long after the last snap.
Every year I watch our seniors leave with offers and options, and I'm proud of that.
The {team} family doesn't stop at graduation. It opens doors for you.
Our alumni answer the phone when a current player needs help getting started. ~ modern
